"C:\\Users\\PC\\AppData\\Local\\Microsoft\\WindowsApps\\Microsoft.PowerShell_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\pwsh.exe" -Command 'Get-Content -Encoding utf8 -Raw src/plc.py; Get-Content -Encoding utf8 -Raw src/modbus.py' in C:\Users\PC\Documents\ChatGPT\에스엘 KDT AI 교육 부트캠프\project
 succeeded in 274ms:
"""LEAK 검사 스테이션 한 곳의 시퀀스를 스캔 주기 상태 기계로 모의한다. 실제 PLC 프로그램이 아니다."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Mapping

from src.modbus import (
    HEARTBEAT_REGISTER, JUDGE_REGISTER, RELEASE_COIL, RESET_COIL, Table, ValueConversionError, to_words,
)


IDLE, CHECK, CLAMP, MEASURE, PUBLISH, WAIT_JUDGE, UNCLAMP, DISCHARGE, ALARM = range(9)
STATE_NAMES = ("IDLE", "CHECK", "CLAMP", "MEASURE", "PUBLISH", "WAIT_JUDGE", "UNCLAMP", "DISCHARGE", "ALARM")

INPUTS = ("part_present", "seated", "direction_ok", "clamp_closed", "clamp_open", "guard_closed", "measure_done")
OUTPUTS = ("clamp_cmd", "measure_cmd", "gate_ok", "gate_ng", "alarm_lamp")

ALARM_CHECK = 11
ALARM_CLAMP = 12
ALARM_MEASURE = 13
ALARM_JUDGE = 14
ALARM_UNCLAMP = 15
ALARM_DISCHARGE = 16
ALARM_HEARTBEAT = 21
ALARM_GUARD = 22
ALARM_CONVERSION = 23
ALARM_CLAMP_CONFLICT = 24
ALARM_MEASURE_SIGNAL = 25

TIMEOUT_ALARMS = {
    CHECK: ("check_scans", ALARM_CHECK),
    CLAMP: ("clamp_scans", ALARM_CLAMP),
    MEASURE: ("measure_scans", ALARM_MEASURE),
    WAIT_JUDGE: ("judge_scans", ALARM_JUDGE),
    UNCLAMP: ("unclamp_scans", ALARM_UNCLAMP),
    DISCHARGE: ("discharge_scans", ALARM_DISCHARGE),
}

REG_STATE, REG_ALARM, REG_MEASURE_ID, REG_VALUE_HIGH, REG_VALUE_LOW, REG_HEARTBEAT, REG_COUNT = range(7)
JUDGE_PASS = 1
JUDGE_FAIL = 2


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Timing:
    heartbeat_scans: int
    timeouts: Mapping[str, int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Snapshot:
    """스캔 시작 때 복사한 값이다. 스캔 중에는 이 값만 본다."""

    inputs: Mapping[str, bool]
    value: str
    reset: bool
    release: bool
    heartbeat: int
    judge: tuple


def interlock(requested, previous, inputs, state):
    """기본 출력에 인터록을 적용한다. 출력 갱신의 마지막 단계다."""
    outputs = dict(requested)
    if outputs["clamp_cmd"] and not previous["clamp_cmd"] and not (
            inputs["seated"] and inputs["direction_ok"] and inputs["guard_closed"]):
        outputs["clamp_cmd"] = False
    if outputs["measure_cmd"] and not (
            inputs["clamp_closed"] and not inputs["clamp_open"] and inputs["guard_closed"]):
        outputs["measure_cmd"] = False
    if outputs["gate_ok"] and outputs["gate_ng"]:
        outputs["gate_ok"] = outputs["gate_ng"] = False
    if not inputs["clamp_open"] or inputs["clamp_closed"]:
        outputs["gate_ok"] = outputs["gate_ng"] = False
    if state == ALARM:
        outputs["measure_cmd"] = outputs["gate_ok"] = outputs["gate_ng"] = False
    return outputs


class Plc:
    """주소표를 입출력으로 삼아 스캔을 돈다. `scan` 한 번이 스캔 하나다."""

    def __init__(self, table: Table, timing: Timing):
        self.table = table
        self.timing = timing
        self.scan_number = 0
        self.state = IDLE
        self.counter = 0
        self.alarm = 0
        self.outputs = {name: False for name in OUTPUTS}
        self.previous_reset = False
        self.previous_release = False
        self.previous_heartbeat = 0
        self.heartbeat_scan = 0
        self.release_latch = False
        self.measure_id = 0
        self.value = ""
        self.verdict = 0
        self.rejected = None
        self.processed = 0
        self.ok_count = 0
        self.ng_count = 0
        self.events = []

    def _snapshot(self, value):
        with self.table.lock:
            return Snapshot(
                dict(zip(INPUTS, self.table.discrete)), value, self.table.coils[RESET_COIL],
                self.table.coils[RELEASE_COIL], self.table.holding[HEARTBEAT_REGISTER],
                tuple(self.table.holding[JUDGE_REGISTER:JUDGE_REGISTER + 2]))

    def _go(self, state):
        self.state = state
        self.counter = 0
        return True

    def _raise(self, code):
        self.alarm = code
        self.events.append("알람 %d" % code)
        return self._go(ALARM)

    def _heartbeat_late(self):
        return self.scan_number - self.heartbeat_scan > self.timing.heartbeat_scans

    def _measure_ready(self, snap):
        return (self.outputs["measure_cmd"] and snap.inputs["clamp_closed"] and not snap.inputs["clamp_open"])

    def _immediate_alarm(self, snap):
        inputs = snap.inputs
        if not inputs["guard_closed"]:
            return ALARM_GUARD
        if self._heartbeat_late():
            return ALARM_HEARTBEAT
        if inputs["clamp_closed"] and inputs["clamp_open"]:
            return ALARM_CLAMP_CONFLICT
        if self.state in (CHECK, CLAMP) and inputs["measure_done"]:
            return ALARM_MEASURE_SIGNAL
        if self.state == MEASURE and inputs["measure_done"] and not self._measure_ready(snap):
            return ALARM_MEASURE_SIGNAL
        return 0

    def _publish(self):
        """변환이 성공하면 판정 지우기, 측정값 기록, ID 증가를 한 번에 한다."""
        try:
            high, low = to_words(self.value)
        except ValueConversionError:
            return self._raise(ALARM_CONVERSION)
        self.measure_id = self.measure_id % 65535 + 1
        with self.table.lock:
            self.table.holding[JUDGE_REGISTER:JUDGE_REGISTER + 2] = [0, 0]
            self.table.input_registers[REG_VALUE_HIGH] = high
            self.table.input_registers[REG_VALUE_LOW] = low
        self.rejected = None
        return self._go(WAIT_JUDGE)

    def _judge(self, snap):
        pair = snap.judge
        if pair == (0, 0):
            return False
        if pair[0] == self.measure_id and pair[1] in (JUDGE_PASS, JUDGE_FAIL):
            self.verdict = pair[1]
            self.events.append("판정 접수")
            return self._go(UNCLAMP)
        if pair != self.rejected:
            self.rejected = pair
            reason = "ID 불일치" if pair[0] != self.measure_id else "판정 값 오류"
            self.events.append("판정 거절: " + reason)
        return False

    def _discharged(self):
        self.processed = (self.processed + 1) % 65536
        if self.verdict == JUDGE_PASS:
            self.ok_count += 1
        else:
            self.ng_count += 1
        self.verdict = 0
        self.value = ""
        return self._go(IDLE)

    def _recoverable(self, snap):
        inputs = snap.inputs
        return (inputs["guard_closed"] and not self._heartbeat_late() and inputs["clamp_open"]
                and not inputs["clamp_closed"] and not inputs["part_present"])

    def _recover(self):
        self.alarm = 0
        self.value = ""
        self.verdict = 0
        self.release_latch = False
        self.events.append("aborted")
        with self.table.lock:
            self.table.holding[JUDGE_REGISTER:JUDGE_REGISTER + 2] = [0, 0]
        return self._go(IDLE)

    def _transition(self, snap, reset_edge):
        inputs = snap.inputs
        state = self.state
        if state == IDLE:
            return inputs["part_present"] and not inputs["measure_done"] and self._go(CHECK)
        if state == CHECK:
            return inputs["seated"] and inputs["direction_ok"] and self._go(CLAMP)
        if state == CLAMP:
            return inputs["clamp_closed"] and self._go(MEASURE)
        if state == MEASURE:
            if inputs["measure_done"]:
                self.value = snap.value
                return self._go(PUBLISH)
            return False
        if state == PUBLISH:
            return self._publish()
        if state == WAIT_JUDGE:
            return self._judge(snap)
        if state == UNCLAMP:
            return inputs["clamp_open"] and self._go(DISCHARGE)
        if state == DISCHARGE:
            return not inputs["part_present"] and self._discharged()
        return reset_edge and self._recoverable(snap) and self._recover()

    def _timeout(self):
        if self.state not in TIMEOUT_ALARMS:
            return
        key, code = TIMEOUT_ALARMS[self.state]
        self.counter += 1
        if self.counter > self.timing.timeouts[key]:
            self._raise(code)

    def _requested(self):
        state = self.state
        outputs = {name: False for name in OUTPUTS}
        outputs["clamp_cmd"] = state in (CLAMP, MEASURE, PUBLISH, WAIT_JUDGE)
        outputs["measure_cmd"] = state == MEASURE
        outputs["gate_ok"] = state == DISCHARGE and self.verdict == JUDGE_PASS
        outputs["gate_ng"] = state == DISCHARGE and self.verdict == JUDGE_FAIL
        if state == ALARM:
            outputs["alarm_lamp"] = True
            outputs["clamp_cmd"] = self.outputs["clamp_cmd"] and not self.release_latch
        return outputs

    def _write(self):
        with self.table.lock:
            self.table.coils[:len(OUTPUTS)] = [self.outputs[name] for name in OUTPUTS]
            registers = self.table.input_registers
            registers[REG_STATE] = self.state
            registers[REG_ALARM] = self.alarm
            registers[REG_MEASURE_ID] = self.measure_id
            registers[REG_HEARTBEAT] = (registers[REG_HEARTBEAT] + 1) % 65536
            registers[REG_COUNT] = self.processed

    def scan(self, value="", after_snapshot=None):
        """스캔 하나를 돈다. after_snapshot은 스냅샷 격리를 확인하는 시험용 훅이다."""
        self.scan_number += 1
        self.events = []
        snap = self._snapshot(value)
        if after_snapshot is not None:
            after_snapshot()
        if snap.heartbeat != self.previous_heartbeat:
            self.previous_heartbeat = snap.heartbeat
            self.heartbeat_scan = self.scan_number
        reset_edge = snap.reset and not self.previous_reset
        release_edge = snap.release and not self.previous_release
        self.previous_reset, self.previous_release = snap.reset, snap.release
        if self.state == ALARM and release_edge:
            self.release_latch = True
        code = 0 if self.state == ALARM else self._immediate_alarm(snap)
        if code:
            self._raise(code)
        elif not self._transition(snap, reset_edge):
            self._timeout()
        self.outputs = interlock(self._requested(), self.outputs, snap.inputs, self.state)
        self._write()
        return self.state

"""소프트웨어 간 Modbus TCP를 표준 라이브러리 소켓으로 구현한다. 실제 PLC, 실제 네트워크 장비가 아니다."""

from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation, localcontext
import socket
import struct
import threading

from src.recipe import is_decimal_text


UNIT_ID = 1
ADDRESS_SPACE = 16
HEADER_BYTES = 6
MIN_LENGTH = 2
MAX_LENGTH = 254

READ_COILS = 0x01
READ_DISCRETE = 0x02
READ_HOLDING = 0x03
READ_INPUT = 0x04
WRITE_COIL = 0x05
WRITE_REGISTER = 0x06
WRITE_REGISTERS = 0x10
FUNCTIONS = (READ_COILS, READ_DISCRETE, READ_HOLDING, READ_INPUT, WRITE_COIL, WRITE_REGISTER, WRITE_REGISTERS)

ILLEGAL_FUNCTION = 1
ILLEGAL_ADDRESS = 2
ILLEGAL_VALUE = 3
DEVICE_FAILURE = 4

COIL_ON = 0xFF00
COIL_OFF = 0x0000
RESET_COIL = 8
RELEASE_COIL = 9
HEARTBEAT_REGISTER = 0
JUDGE_REGISTER = 1
JUDGE_REGISTERS = 2
WRITABLE_COILS = (RESET_COIL, RELEASE_COIL)

VALUE_SCALE = 1000
VALUE_MIN = -2147483648
VALUE_MAX = 2147483647


class ValueConversionError(ValueError):
    """측정값이 정수 천분율 32비트로 정확히 표현되지 않는다."""


class FrameError(ValueError):
    """MBAP 머리가 손상되어 프레임 경계를 믿을 수 없다."""


class ModbusResponseError(ConnectionError):
    """응답이 요청과 맞지 않거나 연결이 끊겼다. 클라이언트는 연결을 닫는다."""


class ModbusTimeout(TimeoutError):
    """응답 제한 시간을 넘겼다."""


class ModbusException(Exception):
    """서버가 형식에 맞는 예외 응답을 돌려줬다. 연결은 유지된다."""

    def __init__(self, function, code):
        super().__init__("function 0x%02X exception %02d" % (function, code))
        self.function = function
        self.code = code


def to_words(text):
    """측정값 문자열을 상위·하위 워드로 바꾼다. 2의 보수이고 상위 워드가 앞선다."""
    if not is_decimal_text(text):
        raise ValueConversionError(str(text) + ": 소수 문자열이 아님")
    with localcontext() as context:
        context.prec = 60
        scaled = Decimal(text) * VALUE_SCALE
        if scaled != scaled.to_integral_value():
            raise ValueConversionError(text + ": 소수 셋째 자리를 넘음")
    number = int(scaled)
    if not VALUE_MIN <= number <= VALUE_MAX:
        raise ValueConversionError(text + ": 32비트 범위를 넘음")
    raw = number & 0xFFFFFFFF
    return raw >> 16, raw & 0xFFFF


def from_words(high, low):
    """워드를 부호 있는 정수로 합친다."""
    raw = high << 16 | low
    return raw - (1 << 32) if raw & 0x80000000 else raw


def restore_value(high, low):
    """워드를 소수 셋째 자리 문자열로 복원한다."""
    number = from_words(high, low)
    sign = "-" if number < 0 else ""
    whole, fraction = divmod(abs(number), VALUE_SCALE)
    return "%s%d.%03d" % (sign, whole, fraction)


class Table:
    """PLC, 모의 설비, 서버가 함께 쓰는 주소표다. 모든 접근은 lock 안에서 한다."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.coils = [False] * ADDRESS_SPACE
        self.discrete = [False] * ADDRESS_SPACE
        self.holding = [0] * ADDRESS_SPACE
        self.input_registers = [0] * ADDRESS_SPACE


def _words(*values):
    return struct.pack(">" + "H" * len(values), *values)


def _pack_bits(bits):
    packed = bytearray((len(bits) + 7) // 8)
    for index, bit in enumerate(bits):
        if bit:
            packed[index // 8] |= 1 << (index % 8)
    return bytes(packed)


def _unpack_bits(packed, count):
    return [bool(packed[index // 8] >> (index % 8) & 1) for index in range(count)]


def _exception(function, code):
    return bytes((function | 0x80, code))


def _read_error(pdu, limit):
    if len(pdu) != 5:
        return ILLEGAL_VALUE
    start, count = struct.unpack(">HH", pdu[1:5])
    if not 1 <= count <= limit:
        return ILLEGAL_VALUE
    if start + count > ADDRESS_SPACE:
        return ILLEGAL_ADDRESS
    return None


def _read_bits(pdu, bits):
    error = _read_error(pdu, 2000)
    if error:
        return _exception(pdu[0], error)
    start, count = struct.unpack(">HH", pdu[1:5])
    packed = _pack_bits(bits[start:start + count])
    return bytes((pdu[0], len(packed))) + packed


def _read_registers(pdu, registers):
    error = _read_error(pdu, 125)
    if error:
        return _exception(pdu[0], error)
    start, count = struct.unpack(">HH", pdu[1:5])
    return bytes((pdu[0], count * 2)) + _words(*registers[start:start + count])


def _write_coil(pdu, table):
    if len(pdu) != 5:
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_VALUE)
    address, value = struct.unpack(">HH", pdu[1:5])
    if value not in (COIL_ON, COIL_OFF):
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_VALUE)
    if address not in WRITABLE_COILS:
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_ADDRESS)
    table.coils[address] = value == COIL_ON
    return pdu


def _write_register(pdu, table):
    if len(pdu) != 5:
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_VALUE)
    address, value = struct.unpack(">HH", pdu[1:5])
    if address != HEARTBEAT_REGISTER:
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_ADDRESS)
    table.holding[address] = value
    return pdu


def _write_registers(pdu, table):
    if len(pdu) < 6:
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_VALUE)
    start, count, size = struct.unpack(">HHB", pdu[1:6])
    if len(pdu) != 6 + size or size != count * 2 or not 1 <= count <= 123:
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_VALUE)
    if start != JUDGE_REGISTER or count != JUDGE_REGISTERS:
        return _exception(pdu[0], ILLEGAL_ADDRESS)
    table.holding[start:start + count] = struct.unpack(">" + "H" * count, pdu[6:])
    return pdu[:5]


def handle_pdu(pdu, table):
    """요청 PDU 하나를 처리해 응답 PDU를 돌려준다. 검증이 끝나기 전에는 쓰지 않는다."""
    function = pdu[0]
    if function not in FUNCTIONS:
        return _exception(function, ILLEGAL_FUNCTION)
    try:
        with table.lock:
            if function == READ_COILS:
                return _read_bits(pdu, table.coils)
            if function == READ_DISCRETE:
                return _read_bits(pdu, table.discrete)
            if function == READ_HOLDING:
                return _read_registers(pdu, table.holding)
            if function == READ_INPUT:
                return _read_registers(pdu, table.input_registers)
            if function == WRITE_COIL:
                return _write_coil(pdu, table)
            if function == WRITE_REGISTER:
                return _write_register(pdu, table)
            return _write_registers(pdu, table)
    except Exception:
        return _exception(function, DEVICE_FAILURE)


class FrameBuffer:
    """길이 기반 수신 버퍼다. 분할·병합 수신을 모두 처리한다."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.data = bytearray()

    def feed(self, chunk):
        self.data.extend(chunk)
        frames = []
        while len(self.data) >= HEADER_BYTES:
            _, protocol, length = struct.unpack(">HHH", self.data[:HEADER_BYTES])
            if protocol != 0 or not MIN_LENGTH <= length <= MAX_LENGTH:
                raise FrameError("MBAP 손상")
            total = HEADER_BYTES + length
            if len(self.data) < total:
                break
            frames.append(bytes(self.data[:total]))
            del self.data[:total]
        return frames


def respond(frame, table):
    """완성된 요청 프레임의 응답 프레임을 만든다. Unit ID가 다르면 None이다."""
    transaction, _, _, unit = struct.unpack(">HHHB", frame[:7])
    if unit != UNIT_ID:
        return None
    pdu = handle_pdu(frame[7:], table)
    return struct.pack(">HHHB", transaction, 0, len(pdu) + 1, unit) + pdu


class ModbusServer:
    """127.0.0.1의 임시 포트에서 연결을 하나씩 받는다."""

    def __init__(self, table):
        self.table = table
        self.listener = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        self.listener.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        self.listener.listen()
        self.listener.settimeout(0.05)
        self.port = self.listener.getsockname()[1]
        self.stopping = threading.Event()
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._accept, daemon=True)
        self.thread.start()

    def _accept(self):
        while not self.stopping.is_set():
            try:
                connection, _ = self.listener.accept()
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except OSError:
                return
            with connection:
                connection.settimeout(None)
                connection.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
                self._serve(connection)

    def _serve(self, connection):
        buffer = FrameBuffer()
        while True:
            try:
                chunk = connection.recv(4096)
            except OSError:
                return
            if not chunk:
                return
            try:
                frames = buffer.feed(chunk)
            except FrameError:
                return
            for frame in frames:
                reply = respond(frame, self.table)
                if reply is not None:
                    try:
                        connection.sendall(reply)
                    except OSError:
                        return

    def close(self):
        self.stopping.set()
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.listener.close()


class ModbusClient:
    """요청을 보내고 응답을 기다린다. on_frame은 완성된 프레임을 방향·기능·16진수로 받는다."""

    def __init__(self, port, timeout=1.0, on_frame=None):
        self.socket = socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), timeout=timeout)
        self.socket.settimeout(timeout)
        self.socket.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
        self.buffer = FrameBuffer()
        self.transaction = 0
        self.on_frame = on_frame or (lambda direction, function, text: None)
        self.closed = False

    def close(self):
        if not self.closed:
            self.closed = True
            self.socket.close()
            self.on_frame("close", 0, "")

    def _fail(self, message):
        self.close()
        raise ModbusResponseError(message)

    def _chunk(self):
        try:
            return self.socket.recv(4096)
        except socket.timeout as error:
            self.close()
            raise ModbusTimeout("응답 제한 시간 초과") from error
        except OSError as error:
            self.close()
            raise ModbusResponseError("수신 실패: " + str(error)) from error

    def _receive(self):
        frames = []
        while not frames:
            chunk = self._chunk()
            if not chunk:
                self._fail("연결 끊김")
            try:
                frames = self.buffer.feed(chunk)
            except FrameError:
                self._fail("응답 MBAP 손상")
        if len(frames) > 1 or self.buffer.data:
            self._fail("요청보다 응답이 많음")
        return frames[0]

    def request(self, pdu):
        """요청 하나를 보내고 머리를 확인한 응답 PDU를 돌려준다. 예외 응답은 ModbusException이다."""
        if self.closed:
            raise ModbusResponseError("연결이 닫혔음")
        self.transaction = self.transaction % 65535 + 1
        frame = struct.pack(">HHHB", self.transaction, 0, len(pdu) + 1, UNIT_ID) + pdu
        self.on_frame("request", pdu[0], frame.hex(" "))
        try:
            self.socket.sendall(frame)
        except OSError as error:
            self.close()
            raise ModbusResponseError("송신 실패: " + str(error)) from error
        reply = self._receive()
        self.on_frame("response", reply[7] if len(reply) > 7 else 0, reply.hex(" "))
        transaction, _, _, unit = struct.unpack(">HHHB", reply[:7])
        body = reply[7:]
        if transaction != self.transaction:
            self._fail("트랜잭션 ID 불일치")
        if unit != UNIT_ID:
            self._fail("Unit ID 불일치")
        if not body:
            self._fail("빈 응답")
        if body[0] == pdu[0] | 0x80:
            if len(body) != 2:
                self._fail("예외 응답 길이 오류")
            raise ModbusException(pdu[0], body[1])
        if body[0] != pdu[0]:
            self._fail("기능 코드 불일치")
        return body

    def read_bits(self, function, start, count):
        body = self.request(_function_with(function, start, count))
        size = (count + 7) // 8
        if len(body) != 2 + size or body[1] != size:
            self._fail("바이트 수 불일치")
        return _unpack_bits(body[2:], count)

    def read_registers(self, function, start, count):
        body = self.request(_function_with(function, start, count))
        if len(body) != 2 + count * 2 or body[1] != count * 2:
            self._fail("바이트 수 불일치")
        return list(struct.unpack(">" + "H" * count, body[2:]))

    def _write_echo(self, pdu):
        if self.request(pdu) != pdu:
            self._fail("에코 불일치")

    def write_coil(self, address, value):
        self._write_echo(_function_with(WRITE_COIL, address, COIL_ON if value else COIL_OFF))

    def write_register(self, address, value):
        self._write_echo(_function_with(WRITE_REGISTER, address, value))

    def write_registers(self, start, values):
        head = _function_with(WRITE_REGISTERS, start, len(values))
        if self.request(head + bytes((len(values) * 2,)) + _words(*values)) != head:
            self._fail("시작 주소·수량 불일치")


def _function_with(function, first, second):
    return bytes((function,)) + _words(first, second)

TIMEOUT_KEYS = ("check_scans", "clamp_scans", "measure_scans", "judge_scans", "unclamp_scans", "discharge_scans")
DELAY_KEYS = ("arrive", "seat", "clamp_close", "measure", "clamp_open", "discharge")

class StationError(ValueError):
    """설정 문제를 위치와 함께 모아서 보고한다. 종료 코드는 3이다."""

    def __init__(self, errors):
        super().__init__("\n".join(errors))
        self.errors = errors


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Scenario:
    state: int = -1
    signal: str = ""
    pc: str = ""


SCENARIOS = {
    "normal": Scenario(),
    "seat_ng": Scenario(CHECK, signal="seated"),
    "clamp_stuck": Scenario(CLAMP, signal="clamp_closed"),
    "judge_lost": Scenario(WAIT_JUDGE, pc="no_judge"),
    "pc_silent": Scenario(MEASURE, pc="no_heartbeat"),
    "disconnect": Scenario(MEASURE, pc="close"),
    "guard_open": Scenario(MEASURE, signal="guard_closed"),
    "stale_judge": Scenario(WAIT_JUDGE, pc="stale"),
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Station:
    generator: Path
    inspection: str
    timing: Timing
    plc_stale_cycles: int
    delays: dict
    post_alarm_cycles: int
    max_cycles: int


def _check_keys(data, allowed, location, errors):
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        errors.append(location + ": 객체여야 함")
        return False
    errors.extend(location + "." + key + ": 없는 키" for key in allowed if key not in data)
    errors.extend(location + "." + key + ": 허용 밖 키" for key in data if key not in allowed)
    return True


def _check_integer(value, location, errors):
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value < 1:
        errors.append(location + ": 1 이상의 정수여야 함")


def load_station(path):
    """설정을 읽어 검증한다. 문제는 위치와 함께 모아서 보고한다."""
    path = Path(path)
    try:
        data = json.loads(path.read_bytes().decode("utf-8-sig"))
    except ValueError as error:
        raise StationError(["$: JSON 오류: " + str(error)]) from error
    errors = []
    _check_keys(data, TOP_KEYS, "$", errors)
    if errors:
        raise StationError(errors)
    for key in ("generator", "inspection"):
        if not isinstance(data[key], str) or not data[key].strip():
            errors.append("$." + key + ": 비어 있지 않은 문자열이어야 함")
    for key in INTEGER_KEYS:
        _check_integer(data[key], "$." + key, errors)
    for name, keys in (("timeouts", TIMEOUT_KEYS), ("delays", DELAY_KEYS)):
        location = "$." + name
        if _check_keys(data[name], keys, location, errors):
            for key in keys:
                if key in data[name]:
                    _check_integer(data[name][key], location + "." + key, errors)
    if errors:
        raise StationError(errors)
    return Station(path.parent / data["generator"], data["inspection"],
                   Timing(data["heartbeat_scans"], dict(data["timeouts"])), data["plc_stale_cycles"],
                   dict(data["delays"]), data["post_alarm_cycles"], data["max_cycles"])


def load_values(station, seed, products):
    """측정 설정의 정상 사건에서 LEAK 최초 측정 value를 제품 순서대로 가져온다."""
    config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(station.generator)
    if "process_cpk" not in config or not is_measured(recipe.models):
        raise ValueError("generator는 측정 설정이어야 함")
    items = {(item.lsl, item.usl): item for model in recipe.models.values() for item in model.inspections
             if item.id == station.inspection}
    if len(items) != 1 or None in next(iter(items)):
        raise ValueError(station.inspection + ": 모든 모델에서 같은 양측 규격이어야 함")
    first = {}
    for row in Generator(seed, config, recipe.models, book).normal_events(products):
        if row["inspection"] == station.inspection and row["value"]:
            first.setdefault(row["product_id"], row["value"])
    if len(first) != products:
        raise ValueError(station.inspection + ": 측정값이 없는 제품이 있음")
    return [first[key] for key in sorted(first)], next(iter(items.values()))


class Plant:
    """모의 설비다. 직전 스캔의 출력과 자기 상태만 보고 입력 비트를 갱신한다."""

    def __init__(self, table, delays, values):
        self.table = table
        self.delays = delays
        self.values = values
        self.product = 0
        self.inputs = {name: False for name in INPUTS}
        self.inputs["clamp_open"] = self.inputs["guard_closed"] = True
        self.raw = ""
        self.timers = {}
        self.forced = {}
        self._publish()

    def _due(self, name, trigger, cycle):
        """트리거를 처음 본 주기를 기억하고, 지연이 지나면 참을 돌려준다. 트리거가 꺼지면 취소한다."""
        if not trigger:
            self.timers.pop(name, None)
            return False
        due = self.timers.setdefault(name, cycle + self.delays[name])
        if cycle >= due:
            del self.timers[name]
            return True
        return False

    def _publish(self):
        merged = {**self.inputs, **self.forced}
        with self.table.lock:
            self.table.discrete[:len(INPUTS)] = [merged[name] for name in INPUTS]

    def update(self, cycle, outputs):
        """트리거는 갱신 전 값으로 본다. 주입은 정상 갱신 뒤에 덮어쓴다."""
        now = dict(self.inputs)
        changes = {}
        if self._due("arrive", not now["part_present"] and self.product < len(self.values), cycle):
            changes["part_present"] = True
        if self._due("seat", now["part_present"] and not now["seated"], cycle):
            changes["seated"] = changes["direction_ok"] = True
        if outputs["clamp_cmd"]:
            changes["clamp_open"] = False
        elif not now["clamp_open"]:
            changes["clamp_closed"] = False
        if self._due("clamp_close", outputs["clamp_cmd"] and not now["clamp_closed"], cycle):
            changes["clamp_closed"] = True
        if self._due("clamp_open", not outputs["clamp_cmd"] and not now["clamp_open"], cycle):
            changes["clamp_open"] = True
        if not outputs["measure_cmd"]:
            changes["measure_done"] = False
            self.raw = ""
        if self._due("measure", outputs["measure_cmd"] and not now["measure_done"], cycle):
            changes["measure_done"] = True
            self.raw = self.values[self.product]
        if self._due("discharge", (outputs["gate_ok"] or outputs["gate_ng"]) and now["part_present"], cycle):
            changes["part_present"] = changes["seated"] = changes["direction_ok"] = False
            self.product += 1
        self.inputs.update(changes)
        self._publish()


class Pc:
    """판정 PC다. 요청마다 응답을 다 받은 뒤 다음으로 넘어간다."""

    def __init__(self, client, item, stale_cycles):
        self.client = client
        self.item = item
        self.stale_cycles = stale_cycles
        self.heartbeat = 0
        self.judged_id = 0
        self.plc_heartbeat = 0
        self.plc_change = 0
        self.stale_noted = False
        self.fault = ""
        self.events = []

    def _exchange(self, cycle):
        if self.fault != "no_heartbeat":
            self.heartbeat = (self.heartbeat + 1) % 65536
            self.client.write_register(HEARTBEAT_REGISTER, self.heartbeat)
        registers = self.client.read_registers(READ_INPUT, 0, 7)
        if registers[REG_HEARTBEAT] != self.plc_heartbeat:
            self.plc_heartbeat = registers[REG_HEARTBEAT]
            self.plc_change = cycle
            self.stale_noted = False
        if cycle - self.plc_change > self.stale_cycles:
            if not self.stale_noted:
                self.stale_noted = True
                self.events.append("PLC 무응답")
            return
        measure_id = registers[REG_MEASURE_ID]
        if registers[REG_STATE] != WAIT_JUDGE or measure_id == self.judged_id or self.fault == "no_judge":
            return
        self.judged_id = measure_id
        value = restore_value(registers[REG_VALUE_HIGH], registers[REG_VALUE_LOW])
        verdict = JUDGE_PASS if judge_value(value, self.item) == "PASS" else JUDGE_FAIL
        self.client.write_registers(JUDGE_REGISTER, [measure_id - 1 if self.fault == "stale" else measure_id, verdict])

    def cycle(self, cycle):
        self.events = []
        if self.fault == "close":
            self.client.close()
        if self.client.closed:
            return
        try:
            self._exchange(cycle)
        except (ModbusResponseError, ModbusException) as error:
            self.events.append("통신 실패: " + str(error))


@dataclass
class Outcome:
    processed: int = 0
    ok_count: int = 0
    ng_count: int = 0
    alarm: int = 0
    heartbeat_scan: int = 0
    records: list = None
    frames: list = None
    target_clamped: bool = False


def _bits(outputs):
    return "".join("1" if outputs[name] else "0" for name in OUTPUTS)


def run_scenario(station, name, values, item, connect=ModbusClient):
    """시나리오 하나를 끝까지 돌린다. 기록은 메모리에 모아 돌려준다."""
    scenario = SCENARIOS[name]
    table = Table()
    plc = Plc(table, station.timing)
    plant = Plant(table, station.delays, values)
    server = ModbusServer(table)
    records, frames = [], []
    cycle = 0

    def log(direction, function, text):
        frames.append({"cycle": cycle, "order": len(frames) + 1, "direction": direction,
                       "function": "%02X" % function, "hex": text})

    client = connect(server.port, SOCKET_TIMEOUT, log)
    pc = Pc(client, item, station.plc_stale_cycles)
    injected = False
    arrived = clamp_on = False
    alarm_cycle = None
    try:
        while plc.processed < len(values) and (alarm_cycle is None or cycle - alarm_cycle < station.post_alarm_cycles):
            if cycle >= station.max_cycles:
                raise ValueError("max_cycles 초과")
            cycle += 1
            plant.update(cycle, plc.outputs)
            plc.scan(plant.raw)
            target = plc.processed + 1 == INJECTED_PRODUCT
            arrived = arrived or (target and plant.inputs["part_present"])
            clamp_on = clamp_on or (arrived and plc.outputs["clamp_cmd"])
            if not injected and target and plc.state == scenario.state:
                injected = True
                pc.fault = scenario.pc
                if scenario.signal:
                    plant.forced[scenario.signal] = False
            events = list(plc.events)
            pc.cycle(cycle)
            events.extend(pc.events)
            records.append({"cycle": cycle, "state": STATE_NAMES[plc.state], "alarm": plc.alarm,
                            "outputs": _bits(plc.outputs), "processed": plc.processed, "events": "; ".join(events),
                            "value": plant.raw})
            if plc.alarm and alarm_cycle is None:
                alarm_cycle = cycle
    finally:
        client.close()
        server.close()
    return Outcome(plc.processed, plc.ok_count, plc.ng_count, plc.alarm, plc.heartbeat_scan, records, frames, clamp_on)


def _write_csv(path, columns, rows):
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=columns)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def run(path, seed, products, names):
    """시나리오를 돌리고, 전부 끝까지 성공했을 때만 보고서를 쓴다."""
    if products < 1:
        raise ValueError("products는 양의 정수여야 함")
    station = load_station(path)
    values, item = load_values(station, seed, products)
    outcomes = {name: run_scenario(station, name, values, item) for name in names}
    directory = Path("reports")
    directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    written = []
    for name, outcome in outcomes.items():
        stem = "plc_station_" + name
        _write_csv(directory / (stem + ".csv"), RECORD_COLUMNS, outcome.records)
        _write_csv(directory / (stem + "_modbus.csv"), MODBUS_COLUMNS, outcome.frames)
        written.extend((directory / (stem + ".csv"), directory / (stem + "_modbus.csv")))
    return outcomes, written


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("station", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--products", type=int, default=20)
    parser.add_argument("--scenario", default="normal", help="시나리오 이름 또는 all")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    names = list(SCENARIOS) if args.scenario == "all" else [args.scenario]
    if any(name not in SCENARIOS for name in names):
        parser.exit(2, "오류: 없는 시나리오 " + args.scenario + "\n")
    try:
        outcomes, written = run(args.station, args.seed, args.products, names)
    except StationError as error:
        parser.exit(3, "스테이션 입력 오류:\n" + "\n".join(error.errors) + "\n")
    except ModbusTimeout as error:
        parser.exit(2, "오류: " + str(error) + ". 결과를 믿을 수 없어 보고서를 쓰지 않음\n")
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, csv.Error, ValueError, KeyError) as error:
        parser.exit(2, "오류: " + str(error) + "\n")
    print("Python 기반 PLC 스캔 모의. 실제 PLC·설비가 아니다.")
    print("scenario | processed | ok | ng | alarm | last_pc_heartbeat_scan")
    for name, outcome in outcomes.items():
        print(" | ".join(map(str, (name, outcome.processed, outcome.ok_count, outcome.ng_count, outcome.alarm,
                                   outcome.heartbeat_scan))))
    print("저장 파일: " + ", ".join(path.as_posix() for path in written))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""LEAK 검사 스테이션 한 곳의 시퀀스를 스캔 주기 상태 기계로 모의한다. 실제 PLC 프로그램이 아니다."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Mapping

from src.modbus import (
    HEARTBEAT_REGISTER, JUDGE_REGISTER, RELEASE_COIL, RESET_COIL, Table, ValueConversionError, to_words,
)


IDLE, CHECK, CLAMP, MEASURE, PUBLISH, WAIT_JUDGE, UNCLAMP, DISCHARGE, ALARM = range(9)
STATE_NAMES = ("IDLE", "CHECK", "CLAMP", "MEASURE", "PUBLISH", "WAIT_JUDGE", "UNCLAMP", "DISCHARGE", "ALARM")

INPUTS = ("part_present", "seated", "direction_ok", "clamp_closed", "clamp_open", "guard_closed", "measure_done")
OUTPUTS = ("clamp_cmd", "measure_cmd", "gate_ok", "gate_ng", "alarm_lamp")

ALARM_CHECK = 11
ALARM_CLAMP = 12
ALARM_MEASURE = 13
ALARM_JUDGE = 14
ALARM_UNCLAMP = 15
ALARM_DISCHARGE = 16
ALARM_HEARTBEAT = 21
ALARM_GUARD = 22
ALARM_CONVERSION = 23
ALARM_CLAMP_CONFLICT = 24
ALARM_MEASURE_SIGNAL = 25

TIMEOUT_ALARMS = {
    CHECK: ("check_scans", ALARM_CHECK),
    CLAMP: ("clamp_scans", ALARM_CLAMP),
    MEASURE: ("measure_scans", ALARM_MEASURE),
    WAIT_JUDGE: ("judge_scans", ALARM_JUDGE),
    UNCLAMP: ("unclamp_scans", ALARM_UNCLAMP),
    DISCHARGE: ("discharge_scans", ALARM_DISCHARGE),
}

REG_STATE, REG_ALARM, REG_MEASURE_ID, REG_VALUE_HIGH, REG_VALUE_LOW, REG_HEARTBEAT, REG_COUNT = range(7)
JUDGE_PASS = 1
JUDGE_FAIL = 2


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Timing:
    heartbeat_scans: int
    timeouts: Mapping[str, int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Snapshot:
    """스캔 시작 때 복사한 값이다. 스캔 중에는 이 값만 본다."""

    inputs: Mapping[str, bool]
    value: str
    reset: bool
    release: bool
    heartbeat: int
    judge: tuple


def interlock(requested, previous, inputs, state):
    """기본 출력에 인터록을 적용한다. 출력 갱신의 마지막 단계다."""
    outputs = dict(requested)
    if outputs["clamp_cmd"] and not previous["clamp_cmd"] and not (
            inputs["seated"] and inputs["direction_ok"] and inputs["guard_closed"]):
        outputs["clamp_cmd"] = False
    if outputs["measure_cmd"] and not (
            inputs["clamp_closed"] and not inputs["clamp_open"] and inputs["guard_closed"]):
        outputs["measure_cmd"] = False
    if outputs["gate_ok"] and outputs["gate_ng"]:
        outputs["gate_ok"] = outputs["gate_ng"] = False
    if not inputs["clamp_open"] or inputs["clamp_closed"]:
        outputs["gate_ok"] = outputs["gate_ng"] = False
    if state == ALARM:
        outputs["measure_cmd"] = outputs["gate_ok"] = outputs["gate_ng"] = False
    return outputs


class Plc:
    """주소표를 입출력으로 삼아 스캔을 돈다. `scan` 한 번이 스캔 하나다."""

    def __init__(self, table: Table, timing: Timing):
        self.table = table
        self.timing = timing
        self.scan_number = 0
        self.state = IDLE
        self.counter = 0
        self.alarm = 0
        self.outputs = {name: False for name in OUTPUTS}
        self.previous_reset = False
        self.previous_release = False
        self.previous_heartbeat = 0
        self.heartbeat_scan = 0
        self.release_latch = False
        self.measure_id = 0
        self.value = ""
        self.verdict = 0
        self.rejected = None
        self.processed = 0
        self.ok_count = 0
        self.ng_count = 0
        self.events = []

    def _snapshot(self, value):
        with self.table.lock:
            return Snapshot(
                dict(zip(INPUTS, self.table.discrete)), value, self.table.coils[RESET_COIL],
                self.table.coils[RELEASE_COIL], self.table.holding[HEARTBEAT_REGISTER],
                tuple(self.table.holding[JUDGE_REGISTER:JUDGE_REGISTER + 2]))

    def _go(self, state):
        self.state = state
        self.counter = 0
        return True

    def _raise(self, code):
        self.alarm = code
        self.events.append("알람 %d" % code)
        return self._go(ALARM)

    def _heartbeat_late(self):
        return self.scan_number - self.heartbeat_scan > self.timing.heartbeat_scans

    def _measure_ready(self, snap):
        return (self.outputs["measure_cmd"] and snap.inputs["clamp_closed"] and not snap.inputs["clamp_open"])

    def _immediate_alarm(self, snap):
        inputs = snap.inputs
        if not inputs["guard_closed"]:
            return ALARM_GUARD
        if self._heartbeat_late():
            return ALARM_HEARTBEAT
        if inputs["clamp_closed"] and inputs["clamp_open"]:
            return ALARM_CLAMP_CONFLICT
        if self.state in (CHECK, CLAMP) and inputs["measure_done"]:
            return ALARM_MEASURE_SIGNAL
        if self.state == MEASURE and inputs["measure_done"] and not self._measure_ready(snap):
            return ALARM_MEASURE_SIGNAL
        return 0

    def _publish(self):
        """변환이 성공하면 판정 지우기, 측정값 기록, ID 증가를 한 번에 한다."""
        try:
            high, low = to_words(self.value)
        except ValueConversionError:
            return self._raise(ALARM_CONVERSION)
        self.measure_id = self.measure_id % 65535 + 1
        with self.table.lock:
            self.table.holding[JUDGE_REGISTER:JUDGE_REGISTER + 2] = [0, 0]
            self.table.input_registers[REG_VALUE_HIGH] = high
            self.table.input_registers[REG_VALUE_LOW] = low
        self.rejected = None
        return self._go(WAIT_JUDGE)

    def _judge(self, snap):
T153–T158은 `src/plc.py`만 호출한다. 표기가 없는 입력은 정상 값이다. 정상 값은 모의 설비 초기값이고, PC 하트비트는 매 스캔 1씩 오른다.

import unittest

from src.modbus import RELEASE_COIL, RESET_COIL, Table
from src.plc import (
    ALARM, CHECK, CLAMP, DISCHARGE, IDLE, INPUTS, MEASURE, OUTPUTS, PUBLISH, REG_COUNT, REG_HEARTBEAT, REG_MEASURE_ID,
    REG_VALUE_HIGH, REG_VALUE_LOW, UNCLAMP, WAIT_JUDGE, Plc, Timing, interlock,
)


TIMEOUTS = {"check_scans": 30, "clamp_scans": 20, "measure_scans": 50, "judge_scans": 30, "unclamp_scans": 20,
            "discharge_scans": 30}


class Rig:
    """정상 입력에서 시작해 스캔마다 PC 하트비트를 올리는 시험 장치다."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.table = Table()
        self.plc = Plc(self.table, Timing(10, TIMEOUTS))
        self.set(clamp_open=True, guard_closed=True)

    def set(self, **signals):
        for name, value in signals.items():
            self.table.discrete[INPUTS.index(name)] = value

    def scan(self, value="", beat=True):
        if beat:
            self.table.holding[0] = (self.table.holding[0] + 1) % 65536
        return self.plc.scan(value)

    def judge(self, judge_id, verdict):
        self.table.holding[1:3] = [judge_id, verdict]

    def reach(self, state):
        """정상 순서로 목표 상태까지 간다."""
        steps = [
            (CHECK, dict(part_present=True)),
            (CLAMP, dict(seated=True, direction_ok=True)),
            (MEASURE, dict(clamp_open=False, clamp_closed=True)),
            (PUBLISH, dict(measure_done=True)),
            (WAIT_JUDGE, dict(measure_done=False)),
            (UNCLAMP, dict()),
            (DISCHARGE, dict(clamp_closed=False, clamp_open=True)),
        ]
        for target, signals in steps:
            self.set(**signals)
            if target == UNCLAMP:
                self.judge(self.plc.measure_id, 1)
            self.scan("10.250" if target == PUBLISH else "")
            if target == state:
                return


def outputs(**fields):
    return {name: fields.get(name, False) for name in OUTPUTS}


def inputs(**fields):
    base = dict(clamp_open=True, guard_closed=True, seated=True, direction_ok=True)
    base.update(fields)
    return {name: base.get(name, False) for name in INPUTS}


class ScanTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t153_snapshot_is_used_for_the_whole_scan(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.table.holding[0] = 1

        def change():
            rig.set(part_present=True)

        rig.plc.scan(after_snapshot=change)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, IDLE)
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), CHECK)

    def test_t154_full_sequence_one_transition_per_scan(self):
        rig = Rig()
        steps = [dict(part_present=True), dict(seated=True, direction_ok=True),
                 dict(clamp_open=False, clamp_closed=True), dict(measure_done=True), dict(measure_done=False),
                 None, dict(clamp_closed=False, clamp_open=True),
                 dict(part_present=False, seated=False, direction_ok=False)]
        states = []
        for number, signals in enumerate(steps, 1):
            if signals is None:
                rig.judge(1, 1)
            else:
                rig.set(**signals)
            states.append(rig.scan("10.250" if number == 4 else ""))
            if number == 5:
                self.assertEqual(rig.table.input_registers[REG_MEASURE_ID], 1)
                self.assertEqual((rig.table.input_registers[REG_VALUE_HIGH], rig.table.input_registers[REG_VALUE_LOW]),
                                 (0x0000, 0x280A))
                self.assertEqual(rig.table.holding[1:3], [0, 0])
        self.assertEqual(states, [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 0])
        self.assertEqual(rig.table.input_registers[REG_COUNT], 1)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.ok_count, 1)

    def test_outputs_follow_each_state(self):
        rig = Rig()
        expected = {CLAMP: "10000", MEASURE: "11000", PUBLISH: "10000", WAIT_JUDGE: "10000", UNCLAMP: "00000",
                    DISCHARGE: "00100"}
        for state, bits in expected.items():
            rig = Rig()
            rig.reach(state)
            self.assertEqual("".join("1" if rig.plc.outputs[name] else "0" for name in OUTPUTS), bits, state)

    def test_ng_verdict_opens_ng_gate_only(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(WAIT_JUDGE)
        rig.judge(1, 2)
        rig.scan()
        rig.set(clamp_closed=False, clamp_open=True)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertTrue(rig.plc.outputs["gate_ng"])
        self.assertFalse(rig.plc.outputs["gate_ok"])
        rig.set(part_present=False)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual((rig.plc.ok_count, rig.plc.ng_count), (0, 1))

    def test_t155_timeout_boundary_and_transition_priority(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.set(part_present=True)
        rig.scan()
        for _ in range(30):
            self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), CHECK)
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), ALARM)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 11)

        rig = Rig()
        rig.set(part_present=True)
        rig.scan()
        for _ in range(30):
            rig.scan()
        rig.set(seated=True, direction_ok=True)
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), CLAMP)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 0)

    def test_every_timeout_has_its_alarm(self):
        for state, key, code in ((CLAMP, "clamp_scans", 12), (MEASURE, "measure_scans", 13),
                                 (WAIT_JUDGE, "judge_scans", 14), (UNCLAMP, "unclamp_scans", 15),
                                 (DISCHARGE, "discharge_scans", 16)):
            rig = Rig()
            rig.reach(state)
            if state == UNCLAMP:
                rig.set(clamp_open=False, clamp_closed=False)
            for _ in range(TIMEOUTS[key]):
                self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), state, key)
            self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), ALARM, key)
            self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, code)

    def test_t156_interlock(self):
        clear = outputs()
        clamped = outputs(clamp_cmd=True)
        self.assertFalse(interlock(clamped, clear, inputs(seated=False), CLAMP)["clamp_cmd"])
        self.assertTrue(interlock(clamped, clamped, inputs(seated=False), MEASURE)["clamp_cmd"])
        measuring = outputs(clamp_cmd=True, measure_cmd=True)
        self.assertFalse(interlock(measuring, clamped, inputs(clamp_open=False), MEASURE)["measure_cmd"])
        self.assertFalse(interlock(measuring, clamped, inputs(clamp_closed=True), MEASURE)["measure_cmd"])
        both = interlock(outputs(gate_ok=True, gate_ng=True), clear, inputs(), DISCHARGE)
        self.assertFalse(both["gate_ok"] or both["gate_ng"])
        self.assertFalse(interlock(outputs(gate_ok=True), clear, inputs(clamp_open=False), DISCHARGE)["gate_ok"])
        self.assertFalse(interlock(outputs(gate_ok=True), clear, inputs(), ALARM)["gate_ok"])
        self.assertTrue(interlock(outputs(gate_ok=True), clear, inputs(), DISCHARGE)["gate_ok"])

    def test_t157_alarm_priority_and_first_alarm_kept(self):
        rig = Rig()
        for _ in range(10):
            rig.scan(beat=False)
        rig.set(guard_closed=False)
        rig.scan(beat=False)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 22)

        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(CLAMP)
        for _ in range(21):
            rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 12)
        rig.set(guard_closed=False)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 12)

        rig = Rig()
        rig.set(clamp_closed=True)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 24)

    def test_t157_measure_signal_alarms(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(CLAMP)
        rig.set(measure_done=True)
        rig.scan("10.250")
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 25)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.value, "")

        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(MEASURE)
        rig.set(measure_done=True, clamp_closed=False)
        rig.scan("10.250")
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 25)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.value, "")

    def test_t157_measure_done_before_command_cannot_pass(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.set(part_present=True)
        rig.scan()
        rig.set(measure_done=True)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 25)

    def test_idle_waits_while_measure_done_is_held(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.set(part_present=True, measure_done=True)
        for _ in range(60):
            self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), IDLE)
        rig.set(measure_done=False)
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), CHECK)

    def test_t157_failed_conversion_keeps_measure_id(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(MEASURE)
        rig.set(measure_done=True)
        rig.scan("1.2345")
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, PUBLISH)
        rig.set(measure_done=False)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 23)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.measure_id, 0)
        self.assertEqual(rig.table.input_registers[REG_MEASURE_ID], 0)

    def _alarm_12(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(CLAMP)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertTrue(rig.plc.outputs["clamp_cmd"])
        for _ in range(20):
            rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 12)
        self.assertTrue(rig.plc.outputs["clamp_cmd"])
        return rig

    def test_t158_recovery_requires_safe_state_and_rising_edge(self):
        rig = self._alarm_12()
        rig.table.coils[RESET_COIL] = True
        rig.set(clamp_closed=True, clamp_open=False)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, ALARM)
        rig.table.coils[RESET_COIL] = False
        rig.scan()

        rig.table.coils[RELEASE_COIL] = True
        rig.scan()
        self.assertFalse(rig.plc.outputs["clamp_cmd"])
        rig.table.coils[RELEASE_COIL] = False
        rig.set(clamp_closed=False, clamp_open=True)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertFalse(rig.plc.outputs["clamp_cmd"])
        rig.table.coils[RESET_COIL] = True
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, ALARM)

        rig.set(part_present=False, seated=False, direction_ok=False)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, ALARM)

        rig.table.coils[RESET_COIL] = False
        rig.scan()
        rig.table.coils[RESET_COIL] = True
        rig.judge(7, 1)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, IDLE)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 0)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.counter, 0)
        self.assertEqual(rig.table.holding[1:3], [0, 0])
        self.assertFalse(rig.plc.release_latch)
        self.assertIn("aborted", rig.plc.events)

    def test_t158_measure_id_is_kept(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(WAIT_JUDGE)
        for _ in range(31):
            rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 14)
        rig.table.coils[RELEASE_COIL] = True
        rig.scan()
        rig.set(clamp_closed=False, clamp_open=True, part_present=False, seated=False, direction_ok=False)
        rig.table.coils[RESET_COIL] = True
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, IDLE)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.measure_id, 1)

    def test_recovery_needs_living_heartbeat(self):
        rig = self._alarm_12()
        rig.table.coils[RELEASE_COIL] = True
        rig.scan()
        rig.set(clamp_closed=False, clamp_open=True, part_present=False, seated=False, direction_ok=False)
        for _ in range(11):
            rig.scan(beat=False)
        rig.table.coils[RESET_COIL] = True
        rig.scan(beat=False)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.state, ALARM)

    def test_manual_release_is_ignored_outside_alarm(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.table.coils[RELEASE_COIL] = True
        rig.reach(CLAMP)
        self.assertTrue(rig.plc.outputs["clamp_cmd"])
        for _ in range(21):
            rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 12)
        self.assertTrue(rig.plc.outputs["clamp_cmd"])

    def test_plc_heartbeat_wraps(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.table.input_registers[REG_HEARTBEAT] = 65535
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.table.input_registers[REG_HEARTBEAT], 0)


class JudgeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t165_measure_id_wraps_to_one(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.plc.measure_id = 65535
        rig.reach(WAIT_JUDGE)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.measure_id, 1)

    def test_t165_rejects_old_id_and_bad_value(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(WAIT_JUDGE)
        rig.judge(rig.plc.measure_id + 1, 1)
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), WAIT_JUDGE)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.events, ["판정 거절: ID 불일치"])
        rig.judge(rig.plc.measure_id, 3)
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), WAIT_JUDGE)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.events, ["판정 거절: 판정 값 오류"])

    def test_t165_same_rejected_pair_is_recorded_once(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(WAIT_JUDGE)
        rig.judge(9, 1)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(len(rig.plc.events), 1)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.events, [])

    def test_t165_judge_written_after_entry_is_kept_and_accepted(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(WAIT_JUDGE)
        self.assertEqual(rig.table.input_registers[REG_MEASURE_ID], 1)
        rig.judge(1, 1)
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), UNCLAMP)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.events, ["판정 접수"])

    def test_old_judge_is_cleared_on_entry(self):
        rig = Rig()
        rig.reach(PUBLISH)
        rig.judge(1, 1)
        rig.set(measure_done=False)
        rig.scan()
        self.assertEqual(rig.table.holding[1:3], [0, 0])
        self.assertEqual(rig.scan(), WAIT_JUDGE)


class HeartbeatTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t166_alarm_after_last_change(self):
        rig = Rig()
        for _ in range(5):
            rig.scan()
        last = rig.plc.scan_number
        for _ in range(10):
            rig.scan(beat=False)
            self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 0)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.scan_number, last + 10)
        rig.scan(beat=False)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 21)

    def test_t166_start_grace(self):
        rig = Rig()
        for _ in range(10):
            rig.scan(beat=False)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 0)
        rig.scan(beat=False)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.alarm, 21)
        self.assertEqual(rig.plc.scan_number, 11)

    unittest.main()

from decimal import Decimal
import socket
import struct
import threading
import unittest

from src.modbus import (
    ModbusClient, ModbusException, ModbusResponseError, ModbusServer, ModbusTimeout, READ_COILS, READ_HOLDING, Table,
    ValueConversionError, from_words, handle_pdu, restore_value, to_words,
)


def request(transaction, pdu, unit=1, protocol=0, length=None):
    size = len(pdu) + 1 if length is None else length
    return struct.pack(">HHHB", transaction, protocol, size, unit) + pdu


def read_pdu(function, start, count):
    return struct.pack(">BHH", function, start, count)


def fc16(start, count, values, size=None):
    body = b"".join(struct.pack(">H", value) for value in values)
    return struct.pack(">BHHB", 0x10, start, count, len(body) if size is None else size) + body


class FrameTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.table = Table()
        self.table.holding[0] = 7
        self.server = ModbusServer(self.table)
        self.addCleanup(self.server.close)

    def connect(self):
        connection = socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", self.server.port), timeout=2)
        connection.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
        self.addCleanup(connection.close)
        return connection

    def receive(self, connection, size=11):
        data = b""
        while len(data) < size:
            chunk = connection.recv(size - len(data))
            if not chunk:
                break
            data += chunk
        return data

    def closed(self, connection):
        try:
            return connection.recv(16) == b""
        except ConnectionError:
            return True

    def test_t159_split_merged_and_truncated(self):
        connection = self.connect()
        for byte in request(1, read_pdu(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)):
            connection.sendall(bytes((byte,)))
        self.assertEqual(self.receive(connection), bytes.fromhex("0001 0000 0005 01 03 02 0007"))

        connection.sendall(request(2, read_pdu(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)) + request(3, read_pdu(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)))
        replies = self.receive(connection, 22)
        self.assertEqual(replies[:2], b"\x00\x02")
        self.assertEqual(replies[11:13], b"\x00\x03")

        connection.sendall(request(4, read_pdu(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1))[:8])
        connection.shutdown(socket.SHUT_WR)
        self.assertTrue(self.closed(connection))

    def test_t160_damaged_frames_close_the_connection(self):
        full = fc16(1, 124, [0] * 124)
        self.assertEqual(len(full) + 1, 255)
        for frame in (request(1, read_pdu(3, 0, 1), protocol=1), request(1, b"\x03", length=1),
                      request(1, b"\x03" * 254), request(1, full)):
            connection = self.connect()
            connection.sendall(frame)
            self.assertTrue(self.closed(connection), frame[:8].hex())

    def test_t160_other_unit_is_dropped_silently(self):
        connection = self.connect()
        connection.sendall(request(1, read_pdu(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1), unit=2))
        connection.sendall(request(2, read_pdu(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)))
        self.assertEqual(self.receive(connection)[:2], b"\x00\x02")


class PduTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t161_exceptions(self):
        cases = [
            (b"\x07", 0x87, 1),
            (read_pdu(3, 16, 1), 0x83, 2),
            (read_pdu(3, 15, 2), 0x83, 2),
            (read_pdu(3, 0, 0), 0x83, 3),
            (read_pdu(3, 0, 126), 0x83, 3),
            (read_pdu(1, 0, 2001), 0x81, 3),
            (struct.pack(">BHHB", 0x10, 1, 124, 248) + b"\x00\x01", 0x90, 3),
            (fc16(1, 2, [1, 1], size=3), 0x90, 3),
            (struct.pack(">BHH", 5, 8, 0x1234), 0x85, 3),
            (struct.pack(">BHH", 5, 0, 0xFF00), 0x85, 2),
            (struct.pack(">BHH", 6, 1, 1), 0x86, 2),
            (b"\x03\x00\x00", 0x83, 3),
            (fc16(0, 123, [1] * 123), 0x90, 2),
            (fc16(2, 1, [1]), 0x90, 2),
            (fc16(0, 3, [1, 1, 1]), 0x90, 2),
            (struct.pack(">BHH", 5, 7, 0xFF00), 0x85, 2),
        ]
        for pdu, function, code in cases:
            table = Table()
            before = (list(table.coils), list(table.holding))
            self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(pdu, table), bytes((function, code)), pdu.hex())
            self.assertEqual((table.coils, table.holding), before)

    def test_t161_internal_error_is_exception_4(self):
        table = Table()
        table.holding = None
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(read_pdu(3, 0, 1), table), b"\x83\x04")

    def test_t162_normal_responses(self):
        table = Table()
        table.coils[0] = table.coils[2] = table.coils[9] = True
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(read_pdu(READ_COILS, 0, 10), table), bytes.fromhex("01 02 05 02"))
        table.discrete[1] = True
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(read_pdu(2, 0, 3), table), bytes.fromhex("02 01 02"))
        table.input_registers[1] = 0xABCD
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(read_pdu(4, 1, 2), table), bytes.fromhex("04 04 ABCD 0000"))

        coil = struct.pack(">BHH", 5, 8, 0xFF00)
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(coil, table), coil)
        self.assertTrue(table.coils[8])
        register = struct.pack(">BHH", 6, 0, 0x1234)
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(register, table), register)
        self.assertEqual(table.holding[0], 0x1234)
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(fc16(1, 2, [3, 1]), table), bytes.fromhex("10 0001 0002"))
        self.assertEqual(table.holding[1:3], [3, 1])

    def test_unassigned_address_reads_zero_and_cannot_be_written(self):
        table = Table()
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(read_pdu(3, 3, 13), table), bytes((3, 26)) + bytes(26))
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(struct.pack(">BHH", 6, 3, 1), table), b"\x86\x02")

    def test_t165_judge_cannot_use_single_register_write(self):
        self.assertEqual(handle_pdu(struct.pack(">BHH", 6, 2, 1), Table()), b"\x86\x02")


class FakeServer:
    """요청 하나마다 reply가 만든 바이트를 돌려준다."""

    def __init__(self, reply):
        self.listener = socket.socket()
        self.listener.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        self.listener.listen()
        self.port = self.listener.getsockname()[1]
        self.reply = reply
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.serve, daemon=True)
        self.thread.start()

    def serve(self):
        connection, _ = self.listener.accept()
        with connection:
            frame = connection.recv(512)
            answer = self.reply(frame)
            if answer:
                connection.sendall(answer)
            connection.recv(16)

    def close(self):
        self.listener.close()


class ClientTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def client(self, reply, timeout=2):
        server = FakeServer(reply)
        self.addCleanup(server.close)
        client = ModbusClient(server.port, timeout)
        self.addCleanup(client.close)
        return client

    def test_t163_mismatched_responses_close_the_connection(self):
        read = lambda client: client.read_registers(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)
        write = lambda client: client.write_register(0, 1)
        cases = [
            (lambda frame: b"\x00\x09\x00\x00\x00\x05\x01\x03\x02\x00\x01", read),
            (lambda frame: frame[:4] + b"\x00\x05\x01\x04\x02\x00\x01", read),
            (lambda frame: frame[:4] + b"\x00\x05\x01\x03\x03\x00\x01", read),
            (lambda frame: frame[:-1] + bytes((frame[-1] ^ 1,)), write),
        ]
        for reply, call in cases:
            client = self.client(reply)
            with self.assertRaises(ModbusResponseError):
                call(client)
            self.assertTrue(client.closed)

    def test_t163_exception_response_keeps_connection(self):
        client = self.client(lambda frame: frame[:4] + b"\x00\x03\x01\x83\x02")
        with self.assertRaises(ModbusException) as caught:
            client.read_registers(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, 2)
        self.assertFalse(client.closed)

    def test_eof_and_timeout(self):
        client = self.client(lambda frame: None, timeout=0.05)
        with self.assertRaises(ModbusTimeout):
            client.read_registers(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)
        self.assertTrue(client.closed)

    def test_transaction_id_wraps_to_one(self):
        table = Table()
        server = ModbusServer(table)
        self.addCleanup(server.close)
        client = ModbusClient(server.port)
        self.addCleanup(client.close)
        client.transaction = 65534
        client.read_registers(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)
        self.assertEqual(client.transaction, 65535)
        client.read_registers(READ_HOLDING, 0, 1)
        self.assertEqual(client.transaction, 1)

    def test_client_round_trip_over_socket(self):
        table = Table()
        server = ModbusServer(table)
        self.addCleanup(server.close)
        frames = []
        client = ModbusClient(server.port, on_frame=lambda *row: frames.append(row))
        self.addCleanup(client.close)
        client.write_coil(8, True)
        client.write_registers(1, [5, 2])
        client.write_register(0, 9)
        self.assertEqual(client.read_bits(READ_COILS, 8, 2), [True, False])
        self.assertEqual(client.read_registers(READ_HOLDING, 0, 3), [9, 5, 2])
        self.assertEqual([row[0] for row in frames[:2]], ["request", "response"])


class ValueTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t164_conversion(self):
        cases = [("10.250", (0x0000, 0x280A)), ("-0.001", (0xFFFF, 0xFFFF)), ("2147483.647", (0x7FFF, 0xFFFF)),
                 ("-2147483.648", (0x8000, 0x0000)), ("1.2300", (0, 1230))]
        for text, words in cases:
            self.assertEqual(to_words(text), words)
            self.assertEqual(from_words(*words), int(Decimal(text) * 1000))
        self.assertEqual(restore_value(0x0000, 0x280A), "10.250")
        self.assertEqual(restore_value(0xFFFF, 0xFFFF), "-0.001")
        self.assertEqual(restore_value(0x7FFF, 0xFFFF), "2147483.647")
        self.assertEqual(restore_value(0x8000, 0x0000), "-2147483.648")

    def test_t164_rejected(self):
        for text in ("2147483.648", "-2147483.649", "1.2345", "", "abc", "NaN", "1e3", None):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueConversionError):
                to_words(text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import copy

    Pc, Plant, SCENARIOS, StationError, load_station, load_values, run_scenario,

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes" / "plc_station.json"
RAW = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def run_cli(*arguments, cwd):
    environment = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT), PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8")
    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "src.station", *map(str, arguments)], cwd=cwd, env=environment,
                          capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8")


def clamp_on(record):
    return record["outputs"][OUTPUTS.index("clamp_cmd")] == "1"


def measure_on(record):
    return record["outputs"][OUTPUTS.index("measure_cmd")] == "1"


def gate_on(record):
    return "1" in record["outputs"][2:4]


class ScenarioTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.station = load_station(CONFIG)
        cls.values, cls.item = load_values(cls.station, 42, 20)
        cls.outcomes = {name: run_scenario(cls.station, name, cls.values, cls.item) for name in SCENARIOS}

    def test_t167_normal(self):
        outcome = self.outcomes["normal"]
        self.assertEqual((outcome.processed, outcome.alarm), (20, 0))

    def test_t167_alarms(self):
        expected = {"seat_ng": 11, "clamp_stuck": 12, "judge_lost": 14, "pc_silent": 21, "disconnect": 21,
                    "guard_open": 22, "stale_judge": 14}
        for name, code in expected.items():
            outcome = self.outcomes[name]
            self.assertEqual((outcome.processed, outcome.alarm), (4, code), name)

    def test_t167_seat_ng_never_clamps_the_fifth_product(self):
        self.assertFalse(self.outcomes["seat_ng"].target_clamped)
        self.assertTrue(any(clamp_on(record) for record in self.outcomes["seat_ng"].records))

    def test_t167_no_discharge_of_the_fifth_product(self):
        for name in ("judge_lost", "pc_silent", "disconnect"):
            fifth = [record for record in self.outcomes[name].records if record["processed"] == 4]
            self.assertFalse(any(gate_on(record) for record in fifth), name)

    def test_t167_guard_open_drops_measure_at_alarm(self):
        records = self.outcomes["guard_open"].records
        alarm = next(index for index, record in enumerate(records) if record["alarm"])
        self.assertTrue(measure_on(records[alarm - 1]))
        self.assertFalse(measure_on(records[alarm]))

    def test_t167_stale_judge_is_rejected_once(self):
        rejected = [record for record in self.outcomes["stale_judge"].records if "판정 거절" in record["events"]]
        self.assertEqual(len(rejected), 1)

    def test_t167_disconnect_records_close_and_stops_traffic(self):
        frames = self.outcomes["disconnect"].frames
        self.assertEqual(frames[-1]["direction"], "close")
        self.assertEqual(sum(frame["direction"] == "close" for frame in frames), 1)

    def test_t169_verdicts_match_judge_value(self):
        outcome = self.outcomes["normal"]
        verdicts = [judge_value(value, self.item) for value in self.values]
        self.assertEqual(outcome.ok_count, verdicts.count("PASS"))
        self.assertEqual(outcome.ng_count, verdicts.count("FAIL"))
        self.assertGreater(outcome.ng_count, 0)

    def test_t169_records_keep_generated_values(self):
        recorded = [record["value"] for record in self.outcomes["normal"].records if record["value"]]
        unique = [value for index, value in enumerate(recorded) if index == 0 or recorded[index - 1] != value]
        self.assertEqual(unique, self.values)

    def test_modbus_log_is_complete_frames_in_order(self):
        frames = self.outcomes["normal"].frames
        self.assertEqual([frame["order"] for frame in frames], list(range(1, len(frames) + 1)))
        for frame in frames:
            if frame["direction"] != "close":
                data = bytes.fromhex(frame["hex"])
                self.assertEqual(len(data), 6 + int.from_bytes(data[4:6], "big"))
        words = bytes.fromhex("%04x%04x" % to_words(self.values[0])).hex(" ")
        self.assertTrue(any(frame["function"] == "04" and words in frame["hex"] for frame in frames))
        judge = next(frame for frame in frames if frame["function"] == "10" and frame["direction"] == "request")
        self.assertEqual(judge["hex"].split()[7:], "10 00 01 00 02 04 00 01 00 01".split())


class PlantTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.table = Table()
        self.plant = Plant(self.table, RAW["delays"], ["10.000"])
        self.off = {name: False for name in OUTPUTS}

    def test_initial_inputs(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.table.discrete[:7], [False, False, False, False, True, True, False])

    def test_delay_counts_from_first_seen_cycle(self):
        self.plant.update(1, self.off)
        self.plant.update(2, self.off)
        self.assertFalse(self.plant.inputs["part_present"])
        self.plant.update(3, self.off)
        self.assertTrue(self.plant.inputs["part_present"])

    def test_clamp_releases_old_sensor_at_once(self):
        on = dict(self.off, clamp_cmd=True)
        self.plant.update(1, on)
        self.assertFalse(self.plant.inputs["clamp_open"])
        self.assertFalse(self.plant.inputs["clamp_closed"])
        self.plant.update(2, on)
        self.plant.update(3, on)
        self.assertFalse(self.plant.inputs["clamp_closed"])
        self.plant.update(4, on)
        self.assertTrue(self.plant.inputs["clamp_closed"])

    def test_dropped_trigger_cancels_the_timer(self):
        on = dict(self.off, measure_cmd=True)
        self.plant.update(1, on)
        self.plant.update(2, self.off)
        for cycle in range(3, 12):
            self.plant.update(cycle, self.off)
        self.assertFalse(self.plant.inputs["measure_done"])

    def test_forced_signal_overrides_plant(self):
        self.plant.forced["guard_closed"] = False
        self.plant.update(1, self.off)
        self.assertFalse(self.table.discrete[5])
        self.assertTrue(self.plant.inputs["guard_closed"])


class StubClient:
    closed = False

    def __init__(self, registers):
        self.registers = registers
        self.writes = []

    def write_register(self, address, value):
        self.writes.append(("heartbeat", value))

    def read_registers(self, function, start, count):
        return list(self.registers)

    def write_registers(self, start, values):
        self.writes.append(("judge", values))


class PcTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.item = load_values(load_station(CONFIG), 42, 1)[1]

    def test_judges_each_measure_id_once(self):
        client = StubClient([5, 0, 3, 0x0000, 0x280A, 1, 0])
        pc = Pc(client, self.item, 20)
        pc.cycle(1)
        client.registers[5] = 2
        pc.cycle(2)
        self.assertEqual([row for row in client.writes if row[0] == "judge"], [("judge", [3, 1])])

    def test_out_of_spec_value_writes_fail(self):
        pc_client = StubClient([5, 0, 1, 0x0000, 0x4E20, 1, 0])
        Pc(pc_client, self.item, 20).cycle(1)
        self.assertEqual(pc_client.writes[-1], ("judge", [1, 2]))

    def test_no_judge_before_wait_state(self):
        client = StubClient([4, 0, 1, 0, 0x280A, 1, 0])
        Pc(client, self.item, 20).cycle(1)
        self.assertFalse(any(row[0] == "judge" for row in client.writes))

    def test_stale_plc_heartbeat_blocks_judgement(self):
        client = StubClient([5, 0, 1, 0, 0x280A, 7, 0])
        pc = Pc(client, self.item, 20)
        pc.cycle(1)
        client.registers[2] = 2
        pc.cycle(22)
        self.assertEqual(pc.events, ["PLC 무응답"])
        pc.cycle(23)
        self.assertEqual(pc.events, [])
        self.assertEqual(sum(row[0] == "judge" for row in client.writes), 1)


class ConfigTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def write(self, folder, mutate):
        data = copy.deepcopy(RAW)
        mutate(data)
        data["generator"] = str(ROOT / "recipes" / "generator_measured.json")
        path = Path(folder) / "station.json"
        path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
        return path

    def test_t170_config_errors_exit_3(self):
        cases = [
            lambda data: data["timeouts"].update(check_scans=-1),
            lambda data: data.pop("max_cycles"),
            lambda data: data.update(extra=1),
            lambda data: data["delays"].update(extra=1),
            lambda data: data["timeouts"].pop("clamp_scans"),
            lambda data: data.update(heartbeat_scans=True),
            lambda data: data.update(timeouts=[]),
        ]
        for mutate in cases:
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
                result = run_cli(self.write(folder, mutate), cwd=folder)
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 3, result.stderr)
                self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports").exists())

    def test_t170_run_errors_exit_2(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            data = copy.deepcopy(RAW)
            data["generator"] = str(ROOT / "recipes" / "generator.json")
            plain = Path(folder) / "plain.json"
            plain.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
            short = self.write(folder, lambda value: value.update(max_cycles=10))
            for arguments in ((plain,), (CONFIG, "--scenario", "nothing"), (short,), (CONFIG, "--products", 0)):
                result = run_cli(*arguments, cwd=folder)
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr)
                self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports").exists())

    def test_t170_load_reports_location(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            path = self.write(folder, lambda data: data["delays"].update(seat=0))
            with self.assertRaises(StationError) as caught:
                load_station(path)
            self.assertIn("$.delays.seat", caught.exception.errors[0])

    def test_t170_silent_server_stops_the_run(self):
        listener = socket.socket()
        listener.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        listener.listen()
        self.addCleanup(listener.close)
        port = listener.getsockname()[1]

        def silent(_, timeout, log):
            return ModbusClient(port, 0.05, log)

        station = load_station(CONFIG)
        values, item = load_values(station, 42, 2)
        with self.assertRaises(ModbusTimeout):
            run_scenario(station, "normal", values, item, connect=silent)

    def test_t170_timeout_exits_2_without_reports(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            before = Path.cwd()
            os.chdir(folder)
            try:
                with mock.patch.object(module, "run_scenario", side_effect=ModbusTimeout("x")):
                    with self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as caught, mock.patch("sys.stderr"):
                        module.main([str(CONFIG)])
            finally:
                os.chdir(before)
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, 2)
            self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "reports").exists())

            run_cli(CONFIG, "--scenario", "normal", cwd=folder)

            self.assertNotIn(str(ROOT), text)
            self.assertNotIn("127.0.0.1", text)

    def test_t171_existing_files_unchanged(self):
        tracked = [path for folder in ("recipes", "data", "reports") for path in (ROOT / folder).rglob("*")
                   if path.is_file() and not path.name.startswith("plc_station")]
        before = {path: path.read_bytes() for path in tracked}
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            self.assertEqual(run_cli(CONFIG, cwd=folder).returncode, 0)
        self.assertEqual({path: path.read_bytes() for path in tracked}, before)
